Bombay, the 17tLi September 1986 
NOTICE 

No. SBD 002767,—In terms of Section 29 of the State 
Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959, the State 
Bank of India, after consulting the Board of Directors of 
the state Bank of Jndore and with the approval of the Re¬ 
serve Bank of India, have appointed Shri Prem Prakash as 
Managing Director of the State Bank of Indore as from 
the 13th September 1986 to 12th September 1989 (inclu¬ 
sive) vice Shri V. Mahadevan. 

Sd./- ILLEGIBLE 
Chairman

THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS OF 
INDIA 

New Delhi-110002, the 19th September 1986 
No. 13-CA(Exam)/N/86,—In continuation of our Notifi¬ 
cation of even number dated 16th July, 1986, it is notified 
that the Chartered Accountants Entrance, Intermediate and 
Final Examinations will be held in November, 1986 also 
at Ambala in HaryaUa. Tho centre is meant for candidates 
who have originally opted for Chandigarh and Ludhiana 
as their centres and who would like to change their centre. 

K. KALYANARAMAN 
Secy. Examination Committee 


EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE CORPORATION 
New Delhi, the 18th September 1986 

No. N.15/13/10/1/86-P&D.—In pursuance of powers 
conferred by Section 46(2) of the Employees’ State Insu¬ 
rance Act, 1948 (34 of 1948), read with Regulation 95-A 
of the Employees' State Insurance (General) Regulations, 
1950, the Director General has fixed the 1-9-1986 as the 
date from which the medical benefits as laid down in the 
said Regulation 95-A and the Orissa Employees’ State 
Insurance (Medical Benefit) Rules, 1951, shall be extended 
to the families of insured persons in the following area in 
the State of Orissa namely :— 

"The area comprising the revenue village of Gangapur 
(PS No. 25 of Jeypore Police Station) under Tehsil of 
Jeypore, Distt. Koraput,” 

HARBHAJAN SINGH 
Director (Pig. & Dev.)

CANTONMENT BOARD, BABINA CANTONMENT 
Babina Cantt., the 5th September 1986 

S.R.O. No. 1 /E/5013.—Whereas tihe Cantonment Board 
Babina proposed to revise rates of Toll Tax as specified in 
the Notification of the Government of India in the Ministry 
of Defence No. S.R.O. 96, dated 9-3-1961, Public notice 
was published by the Cantonment Board vide No. 1/E/ 
5013/1225, dated 11-7-1985, as required under section 61 
of the Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 of 1924), for inviting 
objections and suggestiofas from all persons likely to be 
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of publi¬ 
cation of the said notice; 

And whereas the said notice was put on the Cantonment 
Notice Board on 11th July 1985; 

And whereas no objection or suggestion was received from 
the public by the Cantonment Board, Babina, before the 
said period; 

And whereas the Central Government have duly approv¬ 
ed and confirmed the said draft of the revision of Tedl Tax 
as required under section 63 of the Cantonments Act, 1924 
(2 of 1924); 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 60 of the Cantonments Act, 1924 (2 of 1924), and 


in BUpersession of the Notification published with the Gov¬ 
ernment of India, Ministry oi Defence No. S.R.O. 96 dated 
9-3-1961 save as respect things done or omitted to have 
been done before such supersession, the Cantonment Board, 
Babina, hereby revises with the previous sanction of the 
Central Government Toll Tax in Babina Cantonment, as set 
forth in the Schedule annexed hereto. 

SCHEDULE 
TOLL TAX 

A toll on vehicles laden with goods or passengers entering 
or passing through Babina Cantonment. 


1. Laden Bus 

—Ra. 5-00 

2. Laden Truck 

—Rs. 5-00 

3. Laden Tractor with 
carrier trolly. 

—Rs. 4-00 

4. Laden Matador 

—Rs. 3-00 

5. Laden Advertisement 

Car or vehicle. 

—Rs. 5-00 

6. Laden Auto Rickshaw 

—Re. 1-00 

7. Laden Bullock Cart 

—Re. 0.50 paise 

Provided that no Toll Tax as 
vehicles belonging to— 

aforesaid shall be levied on 


(a) the Government of India or any State Government 
carrying goods certified to be the property of that 
Government; 

(b) the Cantonment Board carrying goods certified to 
be the property of Cantonment Board; 

(c) vehicle carrying solely funeral parties; 

(d) vehicles belonging to persons exempted under sec¬ 
tion 3 of the Tolls (Army and Air Force) Act, 
1901 (2 of 1901). 

(DG Dl&C File No. 53/3/C/L&CI82) 


R. D. CHATURVEDI 
Cantonment Executive Officer, 
Babina Cantonment.
